2026-10-09T16:23:32.8255657Z ##[debug]script=#!/bin/bash
set -o errexit
set -o pipefail
set -x

shopt -s expand_aliases

if [[ -n "$SITE" && "okd4_nprd" =~ "ocp" ]]
then
  app="sicir-frontend-tqs"

  arquivo="/usr/local/bin/oc-v4.13"
  if [ -e "$arquivo" ]; then 
    alias oc="$arquivo"
  fi
elif [[ -n "$SITE" && "$SITE" =~ (okd4|openshift) ]];
then
app="sicir-frontend-tqs"
else
  app="sicir-frontend-tqs-esteiras"
fi

oc version

last_pod=$(oc get pod -l name="$app" -n sicir-tqs -o jsonpath='{range .items[*]}{.metadata.name}{"\n"}' --sort-by=.metadata.creationTimestamp  | tac | grep -v '^$' | head -n1)

echo "Logs do POD: $last_pod"
oc logs $last_pod -c "$app" -n sicir-tqs
